#[inline(hint)]
fn lz4_flex::block::compress::write_integer<'_0, '_1>(output: &'_0 mut lz4_flex::block::Sink<'_1>, n: usize)

    RegionOutlives0: '_1: '_0,
{
    let _0: (); // return
    let output: &'3 mut lz4_flex::block::Sink<'4>; // arg #1
    let n: usize; // arg #2
    let _3: bool; // anonymous local
    let _4: usize; // anonymous local
    let _5: usize; // anonymous local
    let _6: (); // anonymous local
    let _7: &'5 mut lz4_flex::block::Sink<'6>; // anonymous local

    let _10: u8; // anonymous local

    _0 = ()

    // requires 2 branches of bound check (due to the possibility of add overflows)
    // the simple byte at a time implementation bellow is faster in most cases.

        storage_live(_4)

                storage_dead(output)
                unwind_continue
            storage_dead(_7)
            storage_dead(_6)
            storage_dead(_3)
            continue 0
        } else {
            break 0
        }
    }
    storage_dead(_4)
    storage_dead(_3)
    storage_live(_8)
    storage_live(_9)
    _9 = &two-phase-mut (*output)
    storage_live(_10)
    storage_live(_11)
    _11 = copy n
    _10 = cast<usize, u8>(move _11)
    storage_dead(_11)
    _8 = lz4_flex::block::compress::push_byte<'12, '13>(move _9, move _10)
    ↳⚡ storage_dead(_5)
        storage_dead(n)
        storage_dead(output)
        unwind_continue
    storage_dead(_10)

    storage_dead(_8)

    storage_dead(_5)
    storage_dead(n)
    storage_dead(output)

#[inline(hint)]
fn lz4_flex::block::compress::backtrack_match<'_0, '_1, '_2, '_3>(input: &'_0 [u8], cur: &'_1 mut usize, literal_start: usize, source: &'_2 [u8], candidate: &'_3 mut usize)
{

    let input: &'1 [u8]; // arg #1
